**Две версии одной сцены:** Wan 3.0 / Seedance 2.5 — 30 с; Seedance 2.0 — 2 × 15 с. Для части 2 требуется настоящий результат части 1 как @Video1; его пока нет. Первая часть должна закончиться указанным переходным состоянием. Референсы загружаются в указанном порядке. Запуск возможен только по последующей команде, через безлимитный режим с ценой 0 кредитов.

**⚡ Динамичная версия (решение владельца 23.10.2026):** отдельные вкладки «⚡ Динамичная» — те же сюжет, референсы и запреты, но быстрее: действие в каждом такте, склейки на движении, звуке и свете. Прежние вкладки сохранены без изменений; какую версию запускать, решает владелец.

```text
TIMELINE:
[0:00–0:05] AGAINST THE CURRENT. The current rushes away from the sluice, yet the empty boat drives toward it; the rope rips through the dog's paws and he plants his boots, waving a sharp warning to the cat.

[0:05–0:10] THE BOLLARD. The dog whips the rope round the bollard and leans back with all his weight; the boat slews and bumps against the quay.

[0:10–0:15] THE ENVELOPE. The cat drops flat on the towpath and snatches the envelope off the boat floor with one paw while the rope creaks taut.

[0:15–0:20] SECOND LINE. The cat turns the intact red seal into the lantern light, then spots a second, much thinner line at the bow.

[0:20–0:25] TAUT BENEATH. The cat hauls a stretch of the thin line onto the quay; it stays taut and vibrating under the water.

[0:25–0:30] TO THE ARCH. The dog swings the lantern along the line to the dark sluice arch; the cat pockets the unopened envelope and sets off fast along the towpath.

END STATE:
Envelope unopened in the cat's pocket; the fine line runs from the bow under the water toward the dark sluice arch; both follow it; the unseen puller unrevealed.

CAMERA / SPACE:
Wide contradiction shot of current against boat, tight rope action, low snatch shot from the towpath, quick insert on the vibrating thin line, lantern whip to the arch. Cuts land on the rope pull, the bump and the line. Nobody enters the water. Noir energy: cuts land on motion, sound or light; hard lamp shadows sweep the walls; at least one low or silhouette angle. No slideshow zooms, no endless whip-pans, no teleport; geography stays readable.

PERFORMANCE:
Noir tension in motion: every beat carries a clear physical action (striding, crouching, catching, spinning round, running); coats, ears, tails and lantern flames react to speed and wind. Cat stays cool and precise in judgement but moves fast and decisively; dog is anxious, quick and useful, with readable gasps and warning gestures. Serious animated acting: no slapstick, no grins, no idle hold longer than one second, no slow motion.

LIGHTING / MATERIALS:
Painterly animated fur, ink-rich shadows, tactile tweed, wet cobbles and brass; amber gaslight against blue fog. Never photoreal/live-action.

Sluice roar, rope strain, wood knock, envelope rustle; original low woodwind and measured urgent strings. No dialogue, no narration, no sung words. Original music only, no recognizable existing melodies.
```

<a id="scene-19"></a>
## Сцена 19 — Покупатель пустого кресла

<!-- scene-meta: {"target_engine":"Wan 3","production_state":"DRAFT","duration_s":30,"dialogue":{"enabled":false,"language":null},"dependencies":[],"tags":["image_first","owner_requested","draft","not_queued","two_engine_versions","reference_first"]} -->

**Черновик по задаче владельца 21.10.2026. В очередь не добавлять. Генерации не запускались.**

**Что происходит:** На закрытом аукционе маленький ключ отбрасывает огромную тень. Торг выигрывает пустое мокрое кресло, а кот замечает леску у таблички участника.

**Готовность:** Все статичные входы сохранены и проверены; видео ещё не создано, для части 2 понадобится фактический результат части 1. Техническая проверка изображения не является одобрением владельца.

**Назначение:** Сначала создан и отсмотрен кадр, затем по его фактическому содержанию написана сцена. Это заготовка для дальнейшего развития владельцем.

**Входные изображения и их роли:**
<div class="scene-ref-gallery">
<figure><a href="projects/kot-detektiv/references/detective-cat-office-portrait.png" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><img src="projects/kot-detektiv/references/detective-cat-office-portrait.png" alt="Исходная идентичность кота" loading="lazy"></a><figcaption>@Image1 — Исходная идентичность кота</figcaption></figure>
<figure><a href="projects/kot-detektiv/references/detective-cat-police-dog-street.jpg" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><img src="projects/kot-detektiv/references/detective-cat-police-dog-street.jpg" alt="Исходная идентичность пса" loading="lazy"></a><figcaption>@Image2 — Исходная идентичность пса</figcaption></figure>
<figure><a href="projects/kot-detektiv/references/scenes/scene-19/KD19-masked-auction.png" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><img src="projects/kot-detektiv/references/scenes/scene-19/KD19-masked-auction.png" alt="Ключевой кадр: аукцион в масках, ключ под стеклянным колпаком, кот и пёс у лота, тень ключа на стене" loading="lazy"></a><figcaption>@Image3 — Ключевой кадр: аукцион в масках, ключ под стеклянным колпаком, кот и пёс у лота, тень ключа на стене</figcaption></figure>
</div>

**Две версии одной сцены:** Wan 3.0 / Seedance 2.5 — 30 с; Seedance 2.0 — 2 × 15 с. Для части 2 требуется настоящий результат части 1 как @Video1; его пока нет. Первая часть должна закончиться указанным переходным состоянием. Референсы загружаются в указанном порядке. Запуск возможен только по последующей команде, через безлимитный режим с ценой 0 кредитов.

**⚡ Динамичная версия (решение владельца 23.10.2026):** отдельные вкладки «⚡ Динамичная» — те же сюжет, референсы и запреты, но быстрее: действие в каждом такте, склейки на движении, звуке и свете. Прежние вкладки сохранены без изменений; какую версию запускать, решает владелец.

```text
START STATE:
Cat and dog among animal collectors at private auction; small key under glass, owl auctioneer, empty wet velvet chair foreground.

[0:00–0:05] Track from tiny key and oversized physically cast wall shadow to the inexplicably wet empty chair; cat studies droplets closely.

[0:05–0:10] Owl raises auction hammer; a numbered paddle rises behind the empty chair without any visible bidder, animal audience turns.

[0:10–0:15] Cat lowers gaze to paddle base and sees a fine taut thread; dog immediately shifts to watch the nearest doorway.

[0:15–0:20] Cat steps behind the chair and pins thread against the wood with his furry paw; paddle stops halfway through another movement.

[0:20–0:25] Owl suspends the final hammer strike, collectors frozen for one short tense beat; a curtain at far doorway twitches.

[0:25–0:30] Dog moves decisively toward the curtain while cat keeps thread trapped; end before any identity or winning bid is resolved.

END STATE:
Cat pins thread before paddle can move again, owl holds hammer raised; a distant curtain shifts but nobody is identified.

CAMERA / SPACE:
Key scale insert, empty chair reveal, active thread interception and doorway eyeline; rich noir depth.

PERFORMANCE:
Cat calm, precise and decisive; dog anxious but competent, swift useful reactions. Serious animated acting, active deduction, no slapstick or vacant holds.

LIGHTING / MATERIALS:
Painterly animated fur, ink-rich shadows, tactile tweed, wet cobbles and brass; amber gaslight against blue fog. Never photoreal/live-action.

AUDIO:
Quiet animal crowd, hammer poised without strike, thread tension and velvet scrape; original low strings and one metallic note. No dialogue, no narration, no sung words. Original music only, no recognizable existing melodies.

Humans, human background silhouettes, human portraits or skulls, human skin/hands, changed breeds or costumes, photo/live-action look, glossy generic 3D toy style, comedy faces, food gags, pizza, cheese, modern police tape, cars, rotary phones, electric screens, subtitles, readable invented letters, watermarks, revealed culprit face or species, gore, static slideshow.
```

## Сцена 20 — Звон без языка

**Черновик по задаче владельца 21.10.2026. В очередь не добавлять. Генерации не запускались.**

**Что происходит:** Колокол звучал, хотя его язык снят. Кот находит свежий срез каната; очередной удар слышится с другой стороны города.

**Готовность:** Все статичные входы сохранены и проверены; видео ещё не создано, для части 2 понадобится фактический результат части 1. Техническая проверка изображения не является одобрением владельца.

**Назначение:** Сначала создан и отсмотрен кадр, затем по его фактическому содержанию написана сцена. Это заготовка для дальнейшего развития владельцем.

**Входные изображения и их роли:**

**Две версии одной сцены:** Wan 3.0 / Seedance 2.5 — 30 с; Seedance 2.0 — 2 × 15 с. Для части 2 требуется настоящий результат части 1 как @Video1; его пока нет. Первая часть должна закончиться указанным переходным состоянием. Референсы загружаются в указанном порядке. Запуск возможен только по последующей команде, через безлимитный режим с ценой 0 кредитов.

**⚡ Динамичная версия (решение владельца 23.10.2026):** отдельные вкладки «⚡ Динамичная» — те же сюжет, референсы и запреты, но быстрее: действие в каждом такте, склейки на движении, звуке и свете. Прежние вкладки сохранены без изменений; какую версию запускать, решает владелец.

```text
REFERENCES / AUTHORITY ORDER:
@Image1 — Исходная идентичность кота; file: projects/kot-detektiv/references/detective-cat-office-portrait.png.
@Image2 — Исходная идентичность пса; file: projects/kot-detektiv/references/detective-cat-police-dog-street.jpg.
@Image3 — Ключевой кадр: колокольня, кот у верёвки с красной лентой под колоколом без языка, пёс с фонарём; file: projects/kot-detektiv/references/scenes/scene-20/KD20-bell-chamber.png.
Priority: original individual identities first; environment references control only their named locations; action keyframes control staging, never replace identity. Do not import incidental props or people from original references.

CHARACTER APPEARANCE / IDENTITY LOCK:
DETECTIVE CAT: exact original @Image1 identity, grey-brown tabby crown, broad white cheek ruff and muzzle, green analytical eyes, brown tweed deerstalker/cape/waistcoat, white collar and brown tie, watch chain, feline paws. Calm and deliberate, never vacant or lethargic. DOG CONSTABLE: exact dog from @Image2 only, taller brown shepherd-mutt, dark muzzle/ears, navy Victorian bobby coat and helmet, expressive anxious eyes, furred animal paws and tail. His nervous glances and quick notebook gestures contrast with the cat. All characters, background citizens and portraits are anthropomorphic ANIMALS; no humans.

SPECIAL LOCKS:
Present bell never swings or magically rings. Sound comes from another unseen source. No solved culprit or supernatural explanation. Safe platform with railing.

START STATE:
Cat and dog in timber bell chamber, giant stationary bell missing clapper, dust drifting below rim, cut rope with red thread near cat.

TIMELINE:
[0:00–0:05] Follow settling dust toward massive bell underside; dog raises lantern, cat checks cut rope beside support beam.

[0:05–0:10] Reveal clearly empty clapper socket inside bell; cat touches severed rope end and finds fresh pale fibres beneath the red tie.

[0:10–0:15] Both look from the absent clapper to the still bell; dog tests only a loose rope section, no ringing produced.

[0:15–0:20] One resonant note sounds from outside the chamber; cat immediately turns toward the open city-facing louvres.

[0:20–0:25] Dog lifts lantern away from their eyes as cat follows the sound across river toward a different dark tower.

[0:25–0:30] Cat points once, then both move briskly toward the stair; end on silent empty bell foreground and distant answering city.

END STATE:
A new distant bell note comes from across river, both turn toward city window; cat points at a separate dark tower, source unresolved.

CAMERA / SPACE:
Underside reveal, rope detail, sharp motivated turn and broad London vista; serious active deduction.

PERFORMANCE:
Cat calm, precise and decisive; dog anxious but competent, swift useful reactions. Serious animated acting, active deduction, no slapstick or vacant holds.

LIGHTING / MATERIALS:
Painterly animated fur, ink-rich shadows, tactile tweed, wet cobbles and brass; amber gaslight against blue fog. Never photoreal/live-action.

AUDIO:
Timber creak, fine dust fall, one deep distant bell from outside, urgent original low strings. No dialogue, no narration, no sung words. Original music only, no recognizable existing melodies.

NEGATIVE PROMPT:
Humans, human background silhouettes, human portraits or skulls, human skin/hands, changed breeds or costumes, photo/live-action look, glossy generic 3D toy style, comedy faces, food gags, pizza, cheese, modern police tape, cars, rotary phones, electric screens, subtitles, readable invented letters, watermarks, revealed culprit face or species, gore, static slideshow.

FRAME FILL / NO BARS:
Fill the entire generated 16:9 frame edge to edge. No letterbox, pillarbox, black bars, embedded borders, split screens or poster layouts.
```

```text
[0:00–0:05] FALLING DUST. Dust still falling from the bell rim; the dog swings the lantern up under the giant bell while the cat grabs the cut rope beside the support beam.

[0:15–0:20] THE NOTE. One deep note booms from outside the chamber; the cat whips round to the open louvres, dust shaking off the beams.

[0:20–0:25] ACROSS THE RIVER. The dog jerks the lantern away from their eyes; the cat follows the sound across the river to a different dark tower; whip-pan across the city.

[0:25–0:30] THE STAIR. The cat points at the tower and both hurry down the stair; end on the silent empty bell and the answering city.

Cat has pointed at a separate dark tower across the river; both hurry down the stair; the empty bell silent in the foreground; source unresolved.

No slideshow zooms, no endless whip-pans, no teleport; geography stays readable.

PERFORMANCE:
Noir tension in motion: every beat carries a clear physical action (striding, crouching, catching, spinning round, running); coats, ears, tails and lantern flames react to speed and wind. Cat stays cool and precise in judgement but moves fast and decisively; dog is anxious, quick and useful, with readable gasps and warning gestures. Serious animated acting: no slapstick, no grins, no idle hold longer than one second, no slow motion.

LIGHTING / MATERIALS:
Painterly animated fur, ink-rich shadows, tactile tweed, wet cobbles and brass; amber gaslight against blue fog. Never photoreal/live-action.

AUDIO:
Timber creak, fine dust fall, one deep distant bell from outside, urgent original low strings. No dialogue, no narration, no sung words. Original music only, no recognizable existing melodies.
```

```text
@Image3 — Ключевой кадр: колокольня, кот у верёвки с красной лентой под колоколом без языка, пёс с фонарём; file: projects/kot-detektiv/references/scenes/scene-20/KD20-bell-chamber.png.
Priority: original individual identities first; environment references control only their named locations; action keyframes control staging, never replace identity. Do not import incidental props or people from original references.

CHARACTER APPEARANCE / IDENTITY LOCK:
DETECTIVE CAT: exact original @Image1 identity, grey-brown tabby crown, broad white cheek ruff and muzzle, green analytical eyes, brown tweed deerstalker/cape/waistcoat, white collar and brown tie, watch chain, feline paws. Calm and deliberate, never vacant or lethargic. DOG CONSTABLE: exact dog from @Image2 only, taller brown shepherd-mutt, dark muzzle/ears, navy Victorian bobby coat and helmet, expressive anxious eyes, furred animal paws and tail. His nervous glances and quick notebook gestures contrast with the cat. All characters, background citizens and portraits are anthropomorphic ANIMALS; no humans.

SPECIAL LOCKS:
Present bell never swings or magically rings. Sound comes from another unseen source. No solved culprit or supernatural explanation. Safe platform with railing.

START STATE:
Continue @Video1 exactly: Dog's lantern blazes on the empty clapper socket; cat holds the severed rope with fresh fibres; the bell hangs silent.

TIMELINE:
[0:00–0:05] THE NOTE. Continue the actual final frame of Part 1 without replaying it. One deep note booms from outside the chamber; the cat whips round to the open louvres, dust shaking off the beams.

[0:05–0:10] ACROSS THE RIVER. The dog jerks the lantern away from their eyes; the cat follows the sound across the river to a different dark tower; whip-pan across the city.

[0:10–0:15] THE STAIR. The cat points at the tower and both hurry down the stair; end on the silent empty bell and the answering city.

END STATE:
Cat has pointed at a separate dark tower across the river; both hurry down the stair; the empty bell silent in the foreground; source unresolved.

CAMERA / SPACE:
Under-bell up-angle with falling dust, snap insert of the rope fibres, reaction whip on the boom to the louvres, wide London whip-pan, fast follow to the stair. Cuts land on the yank and the boom. The bell itself never swings or rings. Noir energy: cuts land on motion, sound or light; hard lamp shadows sweep the walls; at least one low or silhouette angle. No slideshow zooms, no endless whip-pans, no teleport; geography stays readable.

PERFORMANCE:
Noir tension in motion: every beat carries a clear physical action (striding, crouching, catching, spinning round, running); coats, ears, tails and lantern flames react to speed and wind. Cat stays cool and precise in judgement but moves fast and decisively; dog is anxious, quick and useful, with readable gasps and warning gestures. Serious animated acting: no slapstick, no grins, no idle hold longer than one second, no slow motion.

LIGHTING / MATERIALS:
Painterly animated fur, ink-rich shadows, tactile tweed, wet cobbles and brass; amber gaslight against blue fog. Never photoreal/live-action.

AUDIO:
Timber creak, fine dust fall, one deep distant bell from outside, urgent original low strings. No dialogue, no narration, no sung words. Original music only, no recognizable existing melodies.

NEGATIVE PROMPT:
Humans, human background silhouettes, human portraits or skulls, human skin/hands, changed breeds or costumes, photo/live-action look, glossy generic 3D toy style, comedy faces, food gags, pizza, cheese, modern police tape, cars, rotary phones, electric screens, subtitles, readable invented letters, watermarks, revealed culprit face or species, gore, static slideshow.

FRAME FILL / NO BARS:
Fill the entire generated 16:9 frame edge to edge. No letterbox, pillarbox, black bars, embedded borders, split screens or poster layouts.
```

## Сцена 21 — Событийная заставка сериала — По следу невозможного

<!-- scene-meta: {"target_engine":"Wan 3","production_state":"DRAFT","duration_s":30,"dialogue":{"enabled":false,"language":null},"dependencies":[],"tags":["event_opening","owner_requested","draft","not_queued","two_engine_versions","reference_first"]} -->

**Черновик по задаче владельца 21.10.2026. В очередь не добавлять. Генерации не запускались.**

**Что происходит:** Вокзал → оранжерея → крыши → шлюз → колокольня → название. Заставка знакомит с методичностью кота, быстрыми реакциями пса и огромным загадочным Лондоном; разгадок и кадров трейлера здесь нет.

**Готовность:** Все статичные входы сохранены и проверены; видео ещё не создано, для части 2 понадобится фактический результат части 1. Техническая проверка изображения не является одобрением владельца.

**Назначение:** Событийная начальная заставка сериала: самостоятельный монтаж мира и героев; графическая заставка сохранена отдельно.

**Входные изображения и их роли:**

**Две версии одной сцены:** Wan 3.0 / Seedance 2.5 — 30 с; Seedance 2.0 — 2 × 15 с. Для части 2 требуется настоящий результат части 1 как @Video1; его пока нет. Первая часть должна закончиться указанным переходным состоянием. Референсы загружаются в указанном порядке. Запуск возможен только по последующей команде, через безлимитный режим с ценой 0 кредитов.

**⚡ Динамичная версия (решение владельца 23.10.2026):** отдельные вкладки «⚡ Динамичная» — те же сюжет, референсы и запреты, но быстрее: действие в каждом такте, склейки на движении, звуке и свете. Прежние вкладки сохранены без изменений; какую версию запускать, решает владелец.

<a id="scene-22"></a>
## Сцена 22 — Погоня за тенью — дилижансы ночного Лондона

<!-- scene-meta: {"target_engine":"Seedance 2.0","production_state":"DRAFT","duration_s":30,"dialogue":{"enabled":false,"language":null},"dependencies":[],"tags":["owner_requested","action_chase","draft","not_queued","two_engine_versions","reference_first"]} -->

**Черновик по прямой задаче владельца 21.10.2026. В очередь не добавлять. Видео не запускалось.**

**Что происходит:** По ночным улицам Лондона кот-детектив уверенно правит дилижансом, а паникующий констебль предупреждает об опасном повороте. Они преследуют чёрный дилижанс фигуры в плаще и шляпе с афиши. Колесо проходит рядом с фонарём; погоня вырывается на мост и уходит в туман. Личность беглеца остаётся тайной.

**Контекст использования:** Самостоятельная динамичная сцена для дальнейшей доработки. Это новая погоня, отдельная от сцены 9 с пустой каретой. Основная рекомендуемая версия для движения — Seedance 2.0, 2 × 15 с; альтернативный текст 30 с — Wan 3.0 / Seedance 2.5. Здесь нет команды на запуск.

**Готовность:** Четыре сюжетных PNG созданы и визуально проверены; три исходных опоры сохранены. Все семь изображений приложены ниже. Для части 2 нужен настоящий будущий результат части 1 как @Video1. Изображения и сцена not_approved/DRAFT до решения владельца.

```text
@Image2 — PRIMARY DOG identity/costume only; exclude incidental people and modern props; file: projects/kot-detektiv/references/detective-cat-police-dog-street.jpg.

@Image6 — Right bend at bridge: coach tilt, kerb clearance, wet road and gas lamp; middle action keyframe; file: projects/kot-detektiv/references/scenes/scene-22/KD22-bridge-turn.png.
@Image7 — Rear bridge shot: dog screen-left and cat screen-right, fugitive ahead, final chase geography; file: projects/kot-detektiv/references/scenes/scene-22/KD22-bridge-fog.png.
Priority: original @Image1 cat and @Image2 dog identities override generated staging. @Image3 controls ONLY fugitive silhouette, never their identities or poster layout. @Image4–7 control their named action phases and coherent vehicles. No incidental props from identity references.

CHARACTER APPEARANCE / IDENTITY LOCK:
DETECTIVE CAT: original @Image1 grey-brown tabby crown, broad white cheek ruff and muzzle, green analytical eyes, brown tweed deerstalker, cape and waistcoat, white collar, brown tie, watch chain, white furry paws. Focused and decisive while driving, no redesign. DOG CONSTABLE: dog from @Image2, taller brown shepherd-mutt, dark muzzle and ears, navy Victorian bobby coat and helmet, furry paws, anxious expressive eyes. FUGITIVE: only the distant silhouette from @Image3: dark brimmed hat, raised collar and long black cape with wind-blown hem. Entire face and species stay concealed; do not identify this figure as any portrait suspect. Every sentient character is an anthropomorphic animal, no humans. Horse teams have normal equine anatomy and gallop naturally.

VEHICLE / SEAT / ACTION LOCK — CRITICAL:
Exactly TWO four-wheeled enclosed stagecoaches: HERO burgundy body, ochre wooden wheels, brass lamps, two BAY horses; FUGITIVE black body and wheels, brass lamps, two BLACK horses. Each team has its own complete harness, pole and taut reins. Drivers on the exterior high FRONT box behind their horses, not inside passenger cabins. Cat sits on the vehicle RIGHT, dog on vehicle LEFT: front view cat screen-left/dog right, rear view dog left/cat right. Seats never swap. Fugitive starts about 20 metres ahead in the same direction; no third coach, hidden passengers or duplicate silhouette. Both coaches travel left-to-right before the visible right bend, then away across the bridge. Front axle steers, rear wheels follow, suspension leans; wheels retain ground contact. No collision or horse injury. Reins are held by furred paws, never wrapped around bodies. Keyframes show DIFFERENT phases; animate between them, never hold stills.

START STATE:
Both coaches already galloping along wet London street, black coach about 20 metres ahead to the right, burgundy coach pursuing. Cat on reins, dog braced beside him; bridge turn approaches. @Image4 sets the moving wide staging.

TIMELINE:
[0:00–0:05] PURSUIT ESTABLISHED. Low lateral tracking reveals both full horse teams and coaches travelling left-to-right; black leads, burgundy closes. Hooves strike wet stones, wheels spin and spray fans backward. No introduction pose.

[0:05–0:10] WARNING ON THE BOX. Motivated cut to @Image5 front close-up: dog spots the tight bridge corner and points with a panicked gasp, other paw gripping rail. Cat narrows eyes, leans into motion and takes up the right rein decisively; both faces remain readable.

[0:10–0:15] COMMIT TO THE BEND. Cut to @Image6 exterior geography: black coach turns onto bridge; bay team follows the cat's steering, burgundy front axle rotates right, body loads its springs. Dog brings pointing paw back to brace. End at the exact bridge state, before near wheel clears lamp.

[0:15–0:20] CLEARANCE AT SPEED. Continue the turn without restarting it. Low tracking follows near wheel narrowly clearing kerb and iron lamp, water bursting outward; coach leans on springs but all wheels stay grounded. Cat counterbalances and straightens the team, dog recoils then grips securely.

[0:20–0:25] ONTO THE BRIDGE. Cut on forward motion to @Image7 rear tracking, respecting reversed screen positions. Burgundy coach accelerates along the bridge, black coach and its own horses ahead. Dog sees the dark cape through spray and points urgently; cat maintains pursuit.

[0:25–0:30] INTO THE FOG. Tracking rises gently to show bridge depth; the two teams keep galloping. Fugitive silhouette crosses a gaslight then becomes obscured by mist, two amber lamps remain ahead. End on unresolved moving pursuit, original score still driving.

END STATE:
Both coaches accelerating away across intact bridge; black coach roughly 12 metres ahead enters blue fog, its two amber lamps still traceable. Cat keeps driving and dog points forward. Pursuit continues; no capture, face reveal or fade to a stationary pose.

CAMERA / SPACE:
Four motivated setups: lateral wide, front driver close-up, low exterior bend, rear bridge tracking. Stay on one side of travel axis until the explicit rear cut after the bend. Camera follows real coach speed; no orbit through vehicles, no teleport, no endless whip-pan. Faces sharp during close-up, background and spokes carry selective motion blur.

PERFORMANCE:
Real-time continuous gallop and urgent acceleration; capes, ears, harness and suspension react to inertia. Cat calm in judgement but physically active, constantly steering and balancing. Dog visibly panicking, quick warning gestures and gasps, then useful spotting; no grin, buffoonery or prolonged frozen reaction. No slow motion, idle hero pose, floating carriage or slideshow.

LIGHTING / MATERIALS:
Original painterly ink-rich animation: tactile fur and tweed, worn lacquered wood, wet cobbles, warm brass lamps against cold blue fog. Sharp animated faces, atmospheric London depth. No daylight or live-action photorealism.

AUDIO:
Distinct galloping rhythms, iron tyres on wet cobbles, harness jingle, suspension creak, sharp splashes, wind in capes and dog's startled breath. Original urgent low strings, brass stabs and restrained noir percussion; maintain momentum over cuts. No speech, narration, song or recognizable existing melody.

NEGATIVE PROMPT:
Humans, exposed human skin or hands, revealed fugitive face/species, raven suspect mistaken for fugitive, duplicate cloaked figure on bridge/roof, extra passengers, switched seats, merged coaches or horse teams, extra horse legs, disconnected reins, wheels moving without horses, floating coach, collisions, injured animals, gore, food gags, modern cars/police tape/electric lights, live-action or generic toy 3D, slow motion, static tableau, identity drift, text overlays, subtitles, watermark.

FRAME FILL / NO BARS:
Fill entire 16:9 image edge to edge. No letterbox, pillarbox, black bars, borders, collage, split screen or poster layout.
```
